AT SOURCE | This is the first state a wafer enters. If a carrier is used to supply wafers, wafersare placed at the wafer locations of the carrier.

===== [p46-48] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3.8.1. Loading Operations (9块) =====
--[p46]-- 3.8.1. Loading Operations
The loading operations include the following four patterns that depend on whether the operator access switch or the timer is used as triggers for the start and the completion of loading. These loading operations must meet the following conditions.
: A loading operation method shall be selected from the following four operation patterns, and the selection shall be specified with an equipment constant.
--[p46 [表]]-- (1) When the operator access switch on the loadport operator interface is pressed before a carrier is loaded into the loadport, and pressed again after loading operation is completed:

===== [p155-155] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.5.1. An Error List (3块) =====
--[p155]-- Error | Detailed conditions
Specified control jobs include a process job that should notexist.
(Un) clamping/(un) docking failure | Clamping in the delivery position has failed.
Unclamping in the delivery position has failed.
Docking in the docking position has failed.
Docking in the undocking position has failed.
--[p155]-- Error | Detailed conditions
Double slot and/or cross slot
Carrier ID verification failure | An error has been generated in reading a carrier ID.
Carrier ID data include an error.
Job generation failure | Space available for process job generation is insufficient.
Space available for control job generation is insufficient.
Generation of a process job with an incorrect format hasbeen instructed.
Generation of a control job with an incorrect format hasbeen instructed.

Slot map data are inconsistent.

--[p62]-- Thetransfer, either loading or unloading, can be made either automatically or manually.This state includes two lower states, either of which is used depending on whethera carrier is placed on the loadport.
If a carrier is not placed on a specified loadport when a transition is made toTRANSFER READY, this lower state becomes active. In this state, an externalcarrier or a carrier existing within the equipment (or in the internal buffer) can beloaded to the loadport.

===== [p35-38] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.2.2.2. Representative NPW Operation Models (33块) =====
--[p35]-- Filler | This is the general term used for wafers that are loaded and processedtogether with product wafers to make up for an insufficient number ofproduct wafers in the case of equipment with which it is difficult to achieveproper performance unless multiple wafers are processed at the same time.There are some types that can be reused without being unloaded.Examples: Filler, Dummy
--[p36]-- 5b | Product carrier (containingNPW) | NPW wafers are loaded using a carrier that contains both productwafers and NPW.
6a | Storagemethod | Nonpermanent storage | NPW wafers are stored so that only those required are loaded whennecessary.
6b | Permanent storage | NPW that are not targeted for use with specific product processes arestored permanently. | √
7a | Unloading | Special carrier for NPW | NPW wafers are unloaded using the carrier on which the NPW waferswere loaded. | √
--[p35]-- Stabilizer | This is the general term used for wafers that are individually loaded into theequipment separately from the product wafers in order to help maintain theperformance of the equipment and stabilize the process.In general, these wafers are periodically loaded into the equipment betweenprocessing runs of product wafers and processed separately from productwafers.There are some types that can be reused without being unloaded.Example: Cleaning
--[p38]-- 2d | No automatic recognition | The equipment does not posses a function for automatic recognition.Use takes place in accordance with job instruction from the host.
3a | Trigger forNPW use | Automatic processing byequipment | Instruction from the host is not required; NPW wafers are automaticallyused.
3b | Job specification | NPW wafers are used in accordance with job instruction from the host. | √

10c | Hours of storage insideequipment managed byequipment constant | An event is reported when the cumulative number of hours of storageinside equipment reaches the number of hours set by the equipmentconstant.

5b | Product carrier (containingNPW) | NPW wafers are loaded using a carrier that contains both productwafers and NPW.

--[p49]-- : After an unloading operation is completed, the existence of a carrier shall be handled as the cancellation of unloading processing. For example, a loading operation shall not be performed for a carrier existing.
: Unloading shall be started when the operator access switch is turned on (or carrier is turned off).
: In the automatic access mode, the pressing of the operator access switch shall be neglected.

--[p41]-- 3.7. Carrier Handoff Parallel I/O Interface
The carrier handoff parallel I/O interface indicates a mechanism to transfer state and timing data on loading/unloading to ensure loading/unloading by the automatic conveyor and the equipment. The carrier handoff parallel I/O interface shall meet the following conditions.
· Complied with E84.
: Carrier handoff parallel I/O interface shall be provided in each loadport.

It is when the BUSY is set to ON that theREADY signal must be turned on.

===== [p45-46] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3.8. Loadport Operator Interface (2块) =====
--[p46]-- The equipment must enable manual loading/unloading of carriers through manual operations of the loadport operator interface described below. Outlines of five patterns of loading operations and four patterns of unloading operations are described in Chapter 3.8.1 and Chapter 3.8.2, respectively, together with the changes in the indicators that accompany these operations.
--[p45]-- 3.8. Loadport Operator Interface
This is a mechanism operated by the operator to load and unload the carriers in the manual mode. It is possible to make the sequences in the automatic and manual transfer modes equivalent. A loadport operator interface shall be provided for each loadport. The loadport operator interfaces shall have the following display and operating capabilities.

--[p105]-- 4.7. General Services of High-speed SECS Message Services (HSMS)/Single Session Modes of High-speed SECS Message Services (HSMS-SS)
General services of High-speed SECS message services (HSMS) define interfaces suited for message exchange between computers in a semiconductor factory that use a TCP/IP environment.
--[p108]-- : Only the entity that establishes a TCP/IP connection in the active mode can start the Select procedure. Entities in the passive mode can not start the Select procedure.
: Use of the Deselect procedure is not permitted in the implementation of HSMS-SS. The Separate procedure is used to terminate communications.
--[p107]-- Separate.req has been received or3. The T6 time-out state has been entered by waiting forLinktest.rsp, or4. An HSMS message whose length is less than 10 hasbeen received, or5. An HSMS message whose length is longer than themaximum length supported by the entity has beenreceived, or6. A wrong HSMS message has been received., or7. The T8 time-out state has been entered by waiting forTCP/IP, or8. An uncorrectable TCP/IP error (depending on entities)other than those described above.
--[p105]-- Single session modes of High-speed SECS message services (HSMS-SS) offer standards complementing HSMS, aiming at enabling general HSMS services to replace semiconductor production equipment communications standards 1 (SECS-I).
Equipment must meet the following.
: Equipment must provide functions following E37 HSMS.
: HSMS must use E37.1 HSMS-SS.
: Equipment must enable the setting/modifying of parameters and data must be stored in nonvolatile memory of the equipment.

===== [p26-26] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.14. Collection of Processing Status (2块) =====
--[p26]-- 2.1.1.14. Collection of Processing Status
Processing status indicates the current situations of the equipment.
The equipment status are indicated by:
(1) Wafer processing status in process modules
(2) Carrier status
(3) Wafer status
(4) Control job and process job execution status
(5) Wafer tracking status
By grasping these situations, the host or operator can grasp the current situations of the equipment.
--[p26]-- When a transition occurs among these processing states, the equipment notifies the host or equipment panel of which state the processing has changed to (or what is the tracking target) as an event to allow the host or operator to grasp the situations of the equipment.

===== [p202-202] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.6.1. Monitor (2块) =====
--[p202]-- Reference value for the cumulative number of product wafer processes.
Upper value for the cumulative number of product wafer processes.
Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor>
--[p202]-- 5.1.6.1. Monitor
Monitors are used for verification of the quality of the equipment and products (processing).   
They are employed for detection of dirt, film thickness inspection, etc.
With the exception of the trigger for carrier loading, the basic processing is entirely like the one used for products.
When monitors should be used, the equipment must comply with the following detailed items.
1) The following information must be prepared in the equipment constants.

===== [p28-28] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > (2) Using another carrier (3块) =====
--[p28]-- After the main processing is completed, the preliminary processing wafers and the main processing wafers are joined together outside the equipment.
When preliminary reprocessing is executed, it is necessary to consider the handling of process jobs for preliminary processing.
--[p28]-- While the main processing carrier is left on the loadport, the preliminary processing carrier is taken out of the equipment and measurement work is executed at different points. After that, based on the measurement results, variable parameters are changed in the main processing process program, if required, and the main processing starts.
After the main processing, the processed wafers return to the original carrier.
--[p28]-- (2) Using another carrier
After the preliminary process is executed, the processed wafers are put into a carrier that is different from the original one. Such a carrier must be prepared in advance.
The preliminary processing and the main processing are executed by a single control job. Therefore, process jobs are created separately for the preliminary processing and the main processing. The preliminarily processed wafers are put into another carrier than the carrier for the main processing.

===== [p11-11] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 1.4.2. Structure of Chapters (1块) =====
--[p11]-- 1.4.2. Structure of Chapters
This document consists of the following four chapters (not including this chapter).
: Preconditions (Chapter 2): Environment and requirements assumed in consideration of functions
: Structural Specifications (Chapter 3): Requirements for each equipment structure and their explanations
: Communications Requirements (Chapter 4): Requirements for communications between a host and equipments and their explanations.
: Appendices (Chapter 5): Communications scenarios, message formats etc.

===== [p29-29] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.21. Action to be Taken When Automatic Handoff Has Failed (3块) =====
--[p29]-- 2.1.1.21. Action to be Taken When Automatic Handoff Has Failed
If a carrier handoff abnormality occurs, the equipment and the automatic conveyor interrupt the carrier handoff operation in the abnormal status, waiting for the operator intervention. The operator has to determine whether to continue or cancel the interrupted carrier handoff operation.
: The operator continues the carrier handoff operation.
--[p29]-- When the operator cancels the carrier handoff operation, the production equipment considers that the handoff operation has not been executed or has been completed, depending on the presence of carrier in the loadport.
--[p29]-- When the operator gives the production equipment a direction to continue the handoff operation, the equipment resumes the handoff operation from the status just before the abnormality occurred.
: The operator cancels the carrier handoff operation.
When the operator gives the production equipment a direction to cancel the carrier handoff operation, the equipment initializes the carrier handoff parallel I/O interface signals and terminates the carrier handoff operation.

===== [p28-28] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.20. QTAT Processing (4块) =====
--[p28]-- 2.1.1.20. QTAT Processing
Quick Turn Around Time (QTAT) means manufacture of products in a shorter term. QTAT is required to flexibly meet the customer demands for delivery. QTAT is generally achieved by rearranging the production schedule on the higher level of the equipment (for example, dispatcher). In order to also deal with the work pieces that are already processed by the equipment, it is necessary to achieve QTAT by giving priority to QTAT carriers.
--[p28]-- 1) Whether or not a job for a carrier waiting for processing is started when it gets ready to start processing while a QTAT carrier is being loaded
When a job for a carrier waiting for processing gets ready to start processing while a QTAT carrier is being loaded, the job is executed. This improves the working ratio of the equipment. However, when the QTAT carrier is loaded completely, the carrier needs to wait until the job being executed is interrupted or completed.
--[p28]-- However, in order to resume the job that is interrupted before the job for the QTAT carrier starts, the equipment has to return to the state before the interruption. The equipment may be necessary to execute the pre-processing, depending on the type of equipment.
--[p28]-- Therefore, the QTAT carrier has longer TAT compared to a case in which the QTAT carrier waits until a standby carrier starts processing.
2) Whether or not a job being executed is interrupted when a QTAT carrier is loaded completely
QTAT can be reduced by interrupting the job currently executed and executing a job for a QTAT carrier.

SF | Command name | Descriptions

S14F11 | CJDelete | The deletion of ControlJob is requested.
--[p84]-- S16F27 | CJPause | Processing of ControlJob is suspended.
CJResume | Transition of the ControlJob state from PAUSED to EXECUTINGis requested.
CJCancel | The deletion of a job from waiting queue is requested.
CJStop | The termination of ControlJob is requested.
CJHOQ | Setting a special control job to the next-run control job isrequested.
: Equipment must permit the creation of a ControlJob to control multiple carriers.
: Equipment must manage ControlJob queue states regardless of control modes.
--[p84]-- : Equipment must have a function that, in the online remote mode, the host is informed of the job order status in the queue any time when the upper side requests the status data.
: Equipment must enable that, in the online remote mode, host communication allows job order in the queue to be manipulated (so as to move a specified job to the head of the queue)
: To achieve QTAT processing, it is necessary to prohibit transition from the Queued state to the Selected state.
--[p84 [表]]-- Consequently, the following items must be added to the attributes. In addition, these attribute values must be checked when the transition from the Queued state to the Selected state is made.
Attribute | Type | Comments
Damming | Boolean | Transition from the Queued state to the Selected state is enabled/disabled.
True: State change is disabled. False: State change is enabled. &lt;Default&gt;
· Following checks must be conducted in generating CJ.
: Memory area for generating CJ is available.
: CJ must include specified PJ.
In addition, if an error occurs, the error details must be known from the error code.

===== [p90-90] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.4. Process Job Control (PJM) (1块) =====
--[p90]-- 4.4. Process Job Control (PJM)
The process job defines tracking by specifying process for materials for each process-executing resource. The management of material processing states is performed by specifying materials and processing requirements.

In this state, process-executing resources perform preprocessing, wait for the arrivalof materials, and prepare themselves for material processing. The preprocessingindicates all the operations of the process-executing resources that require recipesprior to the arrival of materials.
WAITING FOR START | This state is only used when the start is made manually.
PROCESSING | This is the state during actual material processing.

ACTIVE | The super-state for the process job in the state for execution.

PAUSED | All the activities are suspended, and the process job waits for the recipe command.

===== [p476-476] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > · Etching device (1块) =====
--[p476]-- · Etching device
A device that etches wafers or the entire surface or a particular part of film generated on the wafers to the desired depth

===== [p208-208] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.6.2. Stabilizer (3块) =====
--[p208 [表]]-- 3) A remote command that enables change of the access status of the stabilizer NPW carrier must be prepared.
Fig. 5-25Stabilizer Process (4/4)
Stabilizer
AMHS | Equipment | Host | Operator | Comment
4a
Product processing
under way
Product processing
completed
Product carrier
unloading
--[p208]-- 5.1.6.2. Stabilizer
Stabilizer wafers are used to stabilize the processing.
Accordingly, one stabilizer wafer is introduced after processing of n-numbers of product wafers, or it is used when switching between processes.
This scenario indicates the use when stabilizer wafers are used just before processing of the first wafer during the idle interval between product wafer processes.
When stabilizers should be used, the equipment must comply with the following detailed items.
--[p208]-- 1) The cumulative times of use must be prepared as wafer object attributes.
2) The following information must be prepared in the equipment constants.
Reference value for the cumulative times of use:
The cumulative times of use that prompt replacement of NPW wafers.
Upper value for the cumulative times of use:
The cumulative times of use that indicate the limit of the service life of NPW wafers.

===== [p81-81] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.3. Control Job Management (CJM) (1块) =====
--[p81]-- 4.3. Control Job Management (CJM)
The control job defines processing by equipment for more than one carrier in the equipment.   
Processing for equipment is defined by more than one process job for materials in carriers.

--[p34]-- 7. Unloading form

===== [p25-25] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.7. Prevention of Mis-operation during Loading/Unloading Carriers (1块) =====
--[p25]-- 2.1.1.7. Prevention of Mis-operation during Loading/Unloading Carriers
To reduce mis-operations where the operator by mistake loads or unloads the carrier in the automatic access mode, clamping/unclamping operations shall change in accordance with the access mode.

===== [p34-34] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2. Method for recognizing the timing for NPW use (2块) =====
--[p34]-- Belonging to automatic recognition by equipment are cases in which an insufficient number of wafers is detected and NPW wafers are committed to supplement the number and cases in which cumulative product wafer processes are calculated and an event is reported when the setting value is reached. In the case of recognition by host, the host gives instructions for NPW processing in accordance with the job.
--[p34]-- 2. Method for recognizing the timing for NPW use
Indicates the method for recognizing the timing for use of NPW wafers. Recognition methods are broadly divided into automatic recognition by the equipment and recognition by the host.

===== [p24-24] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.5. Consecutive Loading/Unloading (1块) =====

===== [p26-26] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.12. Loadport Reservation (3块) =====
--[p26]-- The host verification method is used for the verification of ID.
(3) Loadport reservation is not executed:
Loadport reservation is not executed. The equipment accepts arriving carriers. The host verification method is used for the verification of ID.
--[p26]-- 2.1.1.12. Loadport Reservation
Loadport reservation is a method to prevent the concurrence of loading and unloading actions for the same loadport, and to transfer carriers efficiently by reserving a loadport in advance.
(1) Bind:
Used to establish a relationship between a carrier ID and a loadport. The identification of ID of a carrier conveyed is performed by the equipment.
--[p26]-- If the arriving carrier is the one assumed to arrive, the equipment automatically identifies that the ID is correct, then accepting the carrier. If a carrier other than the one related to the loadport arrives, the carrier data reserved through the Binding operations are overwritten by the newly read carrier ID. Then, the host is asked to check whether the operation should be continued.
(2) ReserveAtPort:
Loadports are reserved, but carrier IDs related to the loadports are not specified.

===== [p40-40] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3.4. Carrier ID Reader (1块) =====
--[p40]-- 3.4. Carrier ID Reader
To check materials while loading a carrier, a carrier ID reader complying with E15.1 shall be provided in the delivery position to read ID data assigned to each carrier (refer to Fig. 3-2 for the delivery position). In addition, the carrier ID reader shall be able to read any sequence of ASCII characters except "\*", "?", "\~," ">" and ":."

--[p57]-- 4.1.6. Error Messages
Equipment must meet requirements for error messages described in E30.

--[p59]-- 4.1.17. Material Transport

===== [p9-9] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 1.1. Purpose (2块) =====
--[p9]-- This document gives the operating specifications that are assumed when the "Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document" is prepared by Semiconductor Leading Technologies (Selete)
--[p9]-- 1.1. Purpose
300 mm semiconductor factories require the construction of an advanced production system in order to raise productivity and deal with wafers of larger diameters. To achieve this, conventional methods are being expanded or improved and new methods are being developed. However, they are not good enough to actually apply to factories and production equipment, so there is a pressing need to consider all the aspects of implementation and operation.

===== [p57-57] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.1.9. Communications Establishment (1块) =====
--[p57]-- 4.1.9. Communications Establishment
Equipment must meet requirements for communications establishment described in E30.

===== [p34-34] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 8. Unload instruction (Only when special NPW carrier is used) (1块) =====
--[p34]-- 8. Unload instruction (Only when special NPW carrier is used)
When unloading using special NPW carrier, this indicates whether the equipment automatically determines the service life limit of NPW wafers and automatically performs the unloading operation or whether unloading does not take place unless there is a service instruction from the host.
Depending on the equipment, both methods may be usable. In this case a check mark should be placed in the Select column.

===== [p30-30] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.24. Action to be Taken When Job Creation Has Failed (5块) =====
--[p30]-- (3) When the host gives a direction to create a process job in a wrong format:
(4) When the host gives a direction to create a control job in a wrong format:
(5) When the host specified control job contains a process job that is not present:
: Continue the carrier processing- The host waits for an external command.
After the information on the host side is modified externally, the host gives the equipment a direction to create the job again according to an external command.
--[p30]-- By positively deleting unnecessary information in the equipment, the host secures an available space in the equipment and gives a direction to create the job again.
: Discontinue the carrier processing- The host cancels the carrier processing. Discomumue ue camci proccssmg- 1ne nost Canccis ue camci proccssmg.
The host requests the equipment to discontinue the carrier processing in which job creation has failed due to insufficient space. The equipment gets ready to unload the carrier.
--[p30]-- Alternatively, the host requests the equipment to discontinue the carrier processing according to an external command, and the equipment gets ready to unload the carrier.
: Discontinue the carrier processing- The host cancels the carrier processing.
The host requests the equipment to discontinue the carrier processing in which job creation has failed due to improper job creation command. The equipment gets ready to unload the carrier.
--[p30]-- 2.1.1.24. Action to be Taken When Job Creation Has Failed
Job creation failure may occur in the following five cases:
(1) When the equipment has no space enough to create a process job:
(2) When the equipment has no space enough to create a control job:
(3) When the host gives a direction to create a process job in a wrong format:
(4) When the host gives a direction to create a control job in a wrong format:
(5) When the host specified control job contains a process job that is not present:
--[p30]-- (1) When the equipment has no space enough to create a process job:
(2) When the equipment has no space enough to create a control job:
: Continue the carrier processing- The host waits until the space is secured in the equipment.
The host checks for available space periodically. When a space required for creating a job becomes available, the host gives a direction to create the job again.
: Continue the carrier processing- The host positively secures the space in the equipment.

===== [p27-27] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.18. Forwarding of Process Job-related Events (2块) =====
--[p27]-- (2) Forwarding events through S6F11:
Process job-related events as well are forwarded to the host through S6F11 (Event Report Send) defined in GEM. It is possible to put events together into a stream function and to use dynamic link reporting.
--[p27]-- 2.1.1.18. Forwarding of Process Job-related Events
In SECS-II (E5), S16F7 (Process Job Alert Notify) and S16F9 (Process Job Event Notify) are defined as stream functions used to forward process job-related events, while, in GEM (E30), S6F11 (Event Report Send) is defined for events.
(1) Forwarding events through S16F and S16F9: Process job-related events are forwarded to the host through S16F7 (Process Job Alert Notify) and S16F9 (Process Job Event Notify) defined in SECS-II (E5).

===== [p27-27] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.16. Process Condition Providing Method (2块) =====
--[p27]-- With variable parameters, process conditions constituting a recipe can be modified temporarily for each execution. Therefore, even if some conditions of a recipe are different, the reproduction of the recipe is unnecessary, because process conditions can be altered by modifying variable parameter values.
--[p27]-- 2.1.1.16. Process Condition Providing Method
Process conditions are specified by using only recipes or both recipes and variable parameters (1) Only recipes are used to specify process conditions:
In producing recipes in advance, the process conditions to be used are assumed. Therefore, recipes must be reproduced if the conditions to be used actually are different from those assumed, however small the difference is.
(2) Both recipes and variable parameters are used to specify process conditions:

===== [p189-189] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.5.6. (Un) Clamping/ (Un) Docking Failures (2块) =====
--[p189]-- 5.1.5.6. (Un) Clamping/ (Un) Docking Failures
Any of (Un) clamping/ (Un) docking operations is not logical and each of them is associated with a physical operation of fixing/releasing. Therefore, an operator must intervene in any failure of this type and judge what measure should be taken.
Operation flows and scenarios to be used when an error occurs are described for the following patterns.
(1) Clamping in a delivery position has failed.
(2) Docking in a delivery position has failed.
--[p189]-- (3) Undocking in a docked position has failed.
(4) Unclamping in a delivery position has failed.

--[p24]-- 2.1.1.2. Ways to Use Carrier ID
Carrier ID shall be used to identify each individual carrier. There are the following two ways of using the ID.

(2) Both writing/reading is performed using a carrier ID writer/reader on loadports

===== [p32-33] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.2.2.1. Definition of NPW (13块) =====
--[p32]-- 2.2.2.1. Definition of NPW
The wafers used in semiconductor fabrication are broadly divided into Product Wafers and NPW (Non-Product Wafers). All other wafers that Product Wafers are broadly defined as NPW.
--[p32]-- The terms used to refer to NPW wafers also differ with the equipment supplier and device maker.
Also, depending on the equipment there are cases in which the use of NPW may be mandatory while there are also equipment types that do not recognize NPW operation.
The purpose of NPW wafers is to maintain the expected performance of the equipment, and the NPW operation should therefore correspond to each piece of equipment
--[p33]-- 2d | No automatic recognition | The equipment does not posses a function for automatic recognition.Use takes place in accordance with job instruction from the host.
3a | Trigger forNPW use | Automatic processing byequipment | Instruction from the host is not required; NPW wafers are automaticallyused.
3b | Job specification | NPW wafers are used in accordance with job instruction from the host.
--[p33]-- 8a | Unloadinginstruction(only whenspecial NPWcarrier isused) | Automatic by equipment | By CarrierComplete when the equipment recognizes that the service lifehas expired.
8b | Host specification | By CarrierComplete following CarrierStatusChange service instructionfrom the host.
9a | NPWreusability | Reusable | Once used NPW wafers can be reused without first being taken out fromthe equipment.
--[p33]-- 10d | Completion of productprocessing | NPW use is completed together with completion of the productprocessing.
10e | No automatic recognition | The equipment possesses no function for automatic recognition.Replacement occurs in accordance with unloading instruction from thehost.
--[p33]-- 4a | NPWprocessingform | During product processing | NPW processing takes place during product processing.
4b | At time other than duringproduct processing | NPW wafers are separately processed
5a | Loadingform | Special carrier for NPW | NPW wafers are loaded using a carrier constructed only for NPW.
5b | Product carrier (containingNPW) | NPW wafers are loaded using a carrier that contains both productwafers and NPW.
--[p33]-- 10b | Cumulative time of usemanaged by equipmentconstant | The equipment constant counts the cumulative time that the NPW waferhas been used inside the equipment, and an event is reported when thelimit value for the time of use is reached
10c | Hours of storage insideequipment managed byequipment constant | An event is reported when the cumulative number of hours of storageinside equipment reaches the number of hours set by the equipmentconstant.
--[p33]-- 9b | Not reusable | Once used NPW wafers must be taken out from the equipment beforethey can be reused.
10a | Method forrecognizingthe time forNPWreplacement(Only in thecase whenNPW wafersare reusable) | Cumulative times of usemanaged by equipmentconstant | The equipment constant counts the cumulative number of times that theNPW wafer has been used inside the equipment, and an event isreported when the limit value for the number of times of use is reached.
--[p32]-- In general, NPW refers to wafers that are periodically loaded into the equipment in order to maintain the processing performance of the equipment, or it refers to wafers that are loaded together with product wafers to make up for an insufficient number of these when the specifications of the equipment demand that multiple wafers must be processed at the same time.
Various models, operation methods, and control methods related to NPW wafers exist.
--[p33]-- 2b | Cumulative time of productwafer processes | To be used when the cumulative time of product wafer processesexceeds the upper number set as reference number in the equipmentconstant. The equipment reports an event.
2c | for NPW use | Automatic recognition byequipment | The equipment automatically determines whether NPW processing isrequired. The equipment reports an event.
--[p32 [表]]-- Consequently, the host or plant operation supervisor should implement NPW operation based on the information required for NPW control provided by the equipment supplier.
The equipment supplier shall prepare written equipment specifications on the operation methods and ways to handle NPW wafers for each of the applicable NPW types mentioned in Table 3-2, Table of NPW Types.
Table 2-2 Table of NPW Types
Item | Variation | Description | Selec-tion
--[p33]-- 1 | NPW type | (NPW designation) | (Description of overview of operation method) | -
2a | Method forrecognizingthe timing | Cumulative number ofproduct wafer processes | To be used when the cumulative number of product wafer processesexceeds the upper number set as reference number in the equipmentconstant. The equipment reports an event.
--[p33]-- 6a | Storagemethod | Nonpermanent storage | NPW wafers are stored so that only those required are loaded whennecessary.
6b | Permanent storage | NPW that are not targeted for use with specific product processes arestored permanently.
7a | Unloadingform | Special carrier for NPW | NPW wafers are unloaded using the carrier on which the NPW waferswere loaded.
7b | Product carrier (containingNPW) | NPW wafers are unloaded using a carrier that contains both productwafers and NPW.

===== [p478-478] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > · Fixed Buffer Equipment (1块) =====
--[p478]-- · Fixed Buffer Equipment
Equipment that has only fixed loadports to store carriers and has no internal buffer so that wafers are directly loaded from and unloaded to the carriers and processed on the loadports.

===== [p24-24] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.3. Access Mode (2块) =====
--[p24]-- 2.1.1.3. Access Mode
Access modes are used to control the means of material (carrier) transfer, convey the intention of the factory to the equipment, and to cause the equipment to execute exclusive transfer processing.
(1) Automatic:
The automatic conveyor transfers (loads/unloads) materials (carriers). The carrier handoff parallel I/O, defined in SEMI E84, shall be used to transfer carriers between the automatic conveyor and manufacturing equipment.
(2) Manual:
--[p24]-- The manual conveyor (including human hands) is used for the transfers (loading/unloading). The CIM guideline 3.2 specifies that, while a carrier is being loaded/unloaded, the manufacturing equipment shall provide handshaking, the same as used to make transfer in automatic handshaking. SEMI Ballot 3098A specifies ways to make transfer through loadport operator interface.

===== [p10-10] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 1.2.1. Type of Buffer (1块) =====
--[p10]-- 1.2.1. Type of Buffer
The equipment buffer is a carrier handling part of production equipment. The SEMI E101-0200 Provisional Guide for Equipment Front End Module (EFEM) Functional Structure Model (FSM) specifies the relationship between the buffers and the buffer functional components. There are two types of basic buffer structures: fixed buffer and internal buffer.
· Fixed buffer equipment:
Contains only the loadport and does not contain the internal buffer to store the carrier.
Wafers to be processed are supplied and collected directly from the carrier by the loadport.

===== [p57-57] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.1.4. Event Notification (3块) =====
--[p57 [表]]-- 4.1.4. Event Notification
Equipment must meet requirements for event notification described in E30.   
The following indicates events assumed in the scope of investigation.
Event name | Standard state model for events
Offline | Control state models
OnlineRemote
OnlineLocal
AlarmOccur
MaterialHasArrived
MaterialHasRemoved &amp; Deleted
MonitorNeeded
MonitorRequired
--[p57]-- : When NPW wafers are to be used as monitors, and the conditions for use of monitor depend on the number of product wafer processes, a MonitorRequired event shall be generated when the reference value for the cumulative number of uses is reached. Also, a MonitorNeeded event shall be generated when the cumulative number of product wafer processes reaches the upper limit value set for cumulative number of uses.
--[p57]-- The reference value for cumulative number of uses indicates the timing at which it is better to confirm the condition by loading monitors. The upper limit value set for the cumulative number of uses indicates the number of times that product wafers may be processed and the timing at which the condition must be confirmed by loading monitors.

===== [p34-35] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 9. NPW reusability (2块) =====
--[p34]-- 9. NPW reusability
Indicates whether an NPW wafer that has been used once can be reused without first being unloaded from the equipment. If reuse is possible, it is desirable that the equipment controls and manages the limit on the time or times of reusability.
10. Method for recognizing the time for NPW replacement (Only in the case when NPW wafers are reusable) Indicates the method for recognizing the end of the service life of and time for replacement of reusable NPW wafers.
--[p35]-- NPW wafers whose service life has expired should be unloaded from the equipment by the procedures indicated in 7. Unloading form and 8. Unload instruction (Only when special NPW carrier is used).

===== [p23-24] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.1. Control Modes in Putting Equipment in Operation and in Ordinary Operations (2块) =====
--[p23]-- 2.1.1.1. Control Modes in Putting Equipment in Operation and in Ordinary Operations
Control modes, defining the level that the host and equipment cooperate in operations, shall be provided for each piece of equipment.
The level of cooperation in each mode shall be as in the following. The same functions shall be provided for each mode.
(1) Online remote:
The host monitors status (alarms and events) of manufacturing equipment and controls the equipment.
(2) Online local:
--[p24]-- The host monitors status (alarms and events) of manufacturing equipment. However, an operator makes control through the equipment panel.
(3) Offline:
An operator monitors status (alarms and events) of manufacturing equipment and controls the equipment. E30 features two types of offline statuses; equipment off-line and host off-line. Unless specifically mentioned, off-line refers to E30 host off-line in this document.

State name | Comments

UNOCCUPIED | Indicates the state that there is no wafer in the wafer location.
OCCUPIED | Indicates the state that there is a wafer in the wafer location.
Table 4-30 Definitions of Wafer Location State Transitions
# | Former state | Triggering item | New state | Comments
1 | UNOCCUPIED | A wafer has been moved to a wafer location | OCCUPIED
2 | OCCUPIED | A wafer has been moved from a wafer location | UNOCCUPIED

===== [p221-221] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.6.3. Filler (3块) =====
--[p221]-- 3) A remote command that enables change of the access status of the Filler (non-permanent) NPW carrier must be prepared.
--[p221]-- 5.1.6.3. Filler
The following indicates the use of fillers in the case of fixed buffer/wafer processing equipment types in which two wafers are processed at a time.
In the case of 2-wafer equipment, a wafer deficit will occur for the last wafer if an odd number of product wafers is processed. Fillers are used to compensate for this deficit.
When fillers should be used, the equipment must comply with the following detailed items.
--[p221]-- 1) The cumulative times of use must be prepared as wafer object attributes.
2) The following information must be prepared in the equipment constants.
Reference value for the cumulative times of use:
The cumulative times of use that prompts replacement of NPW wafers.
Upper value for the cumulative times of use:
The cumulative times of use that indicates the limit of the service life of NPW wafers.

===== [p29-30] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.23. Actions to be Taken When Carrier ID Verification Has Failed (3块) =====
--[p30]-- : To terminate the processing;
Various possible causes, such as an abnormality in the carrier ID location and an abnormality in the carrier ID reader, are checked. The result reveals that the maintenance of the carrier ID as/or the carrier ID reader are necessary, thus forcing the carrier processing.
(2) Actions to be taken when a carrier ID is read but verification by the host generates an error. : To continue the processing:
--[p30]-- The processing is continued even if the carrier ID is different from the one assumed by the host.
: To terminate the processing for the carrier:
Because it is indicated that the carrier is different from the one assumed by the host, the carrier is unloaded without continuing the processing for the carrier.
--[p29]-- 2.1.1.23. Actions to be Taken When Carrier ID Verification Has Failed
The following two cases are considered when carrier ID verification has failed.
(1) A carrier ID cannot be read.
(2) A carrier ID is read, but verification by the host generates an error.
(1) Actions to be taken when a carrier ID cannot be read.
: To continue the processing:
Even if a carrier ID cannot be read, the processing is continued by order of the host. In this case, a carrier ID provided by the host is used.

===== [p19-19] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1. Wafer (1块) =====
--[p19]-- 2.1.1. Wafer
The equipment shall use a 300 mm wafer that conforms to SEMI M1.15.

===== [p10-10] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > : Single-carrier multi-wafer batch: (1块) =====
--[p10]-- : Single-carrier multi-wafer batch:
The equipment that holds one or more wafers within the capacity of a carrier during execution of the main process is considered single-carrier batch equipment. An example is the injector
that processes multiple wafers on the disc in a batch.
· Multi-carrier batch:
The equipment that holds wafers exceeding the capacity of a carrier during execution of the main process is considered multi-carrier batch equipment.
Examples are horizontal LPCVD furnace equipment, the vertical CVD reactor and the wet bench

===== [p27-28] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.19. Preliminary Processing (2块) =====
--[p28]-- If certain process results cannot be obtained from the preliminary processing, the processing will be repeated. This is called "preliminary reprocessing." The following two patterns of preliminary processing are available.
--[p27]-- 2.1.1.19. Preliminary Processing
When one or more wafers are processed in advance to determine the processing conditions for the remaining wafers, the wafer processing that is executed in advance and the confirmation (inspection) of the results of that processing are called "preliminary processing." Determination of the processing conditions for the remaining wafers according to the results of the preliminary processing is called "main processing."

===== [p29-29] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.22. Actions to be Taken When Slot Map Verification Has Failed (5块) =====
--[p29]-- 2.1.1.22. Actions to be Taken When Slot Map Verification Has Failed
Even if a slot map verification has failed, providing the equipment with wafer data from the host as the content map makes the continuation of the processing possible. However, if an error is generated, the operator should check the states of wafers in the carrier.
(1) If reading a slot map has failed:
: Suspend the carrier processing and put it in the state where unloading is possible.
--[p29]-- To enable the operator to check the states of wafers in the carrier, the carrier processing by the equipment must be suspended and the carrier must be put in the state where unloading is possible.
: The host provides the slot map data and the carrier processing is continued. Providing wafer data recognized by the host enables the continuation of the processing.
(2) If slot map data are different for those held by the host:
--[p29]-- : Suspend the carrier processing and put it in the state where unloading is possible. To enable the operator to check the states of wafers in the carrier, the carrier processing by the equipment must be suspended and the carrier must be put in the state where unloading is possible.
: The host-side data are replaced with actual ones and the carrier processing is continued. Providing wafer data recognized by the host enables the continuation of the processing.
--[p29]-- (3) If cross slot or double slot is detected
: Suspend the carrier processing and put it in the state where unloading is possible.
To enable the operator to check the states of wafers in the carrier, the carrier processing by the equipment must be suspended and the carrier must be put in the state where unloading is possible.
: The host-side data are modified so that wafers in an abnormal state are not processed. Then, the processing is continued.
--[p29]-- Providing wafer data in the state where wafer data for slots (where double slot or cross slot is generated) have been deleted makes the continuation of the processing possible.

The equipment panel indicates the console panel that operators use to operate the equipment, and shall be provided on the front and rear sides. ===== [p31-31] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.25. Action to be Taken When Clamp/Dock/Undock/Unclamp Has Failed (2块) =====
--[p31]-- (1) Clamping has failed.
(2) Docking has failed.
(3) Undocking has failed.
(4) Unclamping has failed.
If one of these abnormalities occurs, there is no other way than the one that an operator intervenes to perform maintenance. The carrier and the equipment are checked, with the abnormal situation kept intact, to repair or make adjustments for the failed portion.
--[p31]-- 2.1.1.25. Action to be Taken When Clamp/Dock/Undock/Unclamp Has Failed
Clamping is a process executed by the equipment to secure a carrier to a loadport. Docking is a process executed by the equipment to push a carrier against the FIMS surface. Undocking is a process to remove a carrier from the FIMS surface. Unclamping is a process to release a clamped carrier to unload it from the loadport. All of these processes are physical operations to secure and release carriers, not logical operations.

===== [p19-19] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.2. Carrier (1块) =====
--[p19]-- 2.1.2. Carrier
Carriers of the open type and of the Front Opening Unified Pod (FOUP) type are available for 300 mm wafer carriers. FOUP has a built-in cassette (which is usually integrated and not removable) and a front opening door. FOUP requires the door opening mechanism, as it loads and unloads wafers on the same point in the loadport. The open cassette type requires no special docking or opening mechanism.
The equipment shall have carriers of the FOUP type that are designed to hold 25 wafers and conform to SEMI E47.1.

===== [p61-61] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.2. Carrier Management Standards (CMS) (1块) =====
--[p61]-- 4.2. Carrier Management Standards (CMS)
The carrier management standards are provided to the host to have information about carrier transport in the automatic and manual modes and to play its role more easily. Consequently, the following state models are defined.
: Carrier transport between AMHS equipment and production equipment.
: Selective setting a loadport-accessing mode
: Loadport-reserved state
: States associating loadports with carriers
: Carrier ID verification, carrier slot map verification and carrier-accessing states

===== [p11-11] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 1.4.1. Organization of Document (1块) =====
--[p11]-- 1.4.1. Organization of Document
This document provides requirement specifications for online use of the manufacturing equipment, and is issued for each type of equipment. The contents are intended for use as a base of purchasing specifications when device manufacturers purchase manufacturing equipment. This document describes requirements for the Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor.

===== [p40-40] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3.6. Clamping Mechanism (2块) =====
--[p40]-- : In unloading in the automatic access mode, the equipment shall set the clamping mechanism to the unclamping state after the processing of the carrier is completed. Then, the mechanism shall be set to the unclamping state when a handshaking operation through the carrier handoff parallel I/O interface is initiated.
: In unloading in the manual access mode, the equipment shall set the clamping mechanism to the unclamping state after the processing of the carrier is completed.
--[p40]-- 3.6. Clamping Mechanism
The clamping mechanism indicates a mechanism to fix a carrier to the equipment. A clamping mechanism shall meet the following conditions.
: A carrier shall be automatically clamped in the delivery position and the dock position.
: In loading, the equipment shall set the clamping mechanism to the unclamping state when there is no carrier and regardless of the access mode.

--[p33]-- 1. NPW type
Describes the designations of the NPW wafers that can be processed by the equipment and gives an overview of these. Filler, Stabilizer, and Monitor are examples of representative NPW wafers.

===== [p162-162] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.5.3. Slot Map Verification Failure (2块) =====
--[p162]-- 5.1.5.3. Slot Map Verification Failure
Even if a slot map verification has failed, the continuation of processing is possible by providing to equipment wafer information from the host as a content map. However, if an error occurs, the operator should check the wafer state within the carrier. Therefore, operators shall check wafer states within carriers in any situation.
Operation flows and their scenarios to be used when an error occurs are described for the following three patterns.
--[p162]-- (1) Slot map reading failure
(2) Slot map data error
(3) Double slot and cross slot

===== [p26-26] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.13. Slot Map Verification (2块) =====
--[p26]-- 2.1.1.13. Slot Map Verification
Slot map verification means that when a carrier is loaded, the slot map reader reads whether wafers are present in the individual slots of the carrier and compares the result with the wafer presence information for the individual slots recognized by the host or the operator, in order to judge whether or not the wafers to be processed are present in the correct slots.
--[p26]-- There are, like for the carrier ID verification, the following two methods, equipment verification and host verification, are available.
(1) Equipment verification:
The host informs the equipment of a slot map to be used for a carrier in advance. If the slot map of an arrived carrier is the same as the one informed by the host, the equipment identifies that the slot map is correct, accepting the carrier.
(2) Host verification:
In this method, the host does not inform the equipment of slot maps.
A slot map read by the equipment is sent to the host, which decides whether the carrier should be accepted.

===== [p27-27] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.15. Wafer Identifier Providing Method (2块) =====
--[p27]-- 2.1.1.15. Wafer Identifier Providing Method
Wafer identifying information used by the production equipment refers to wafer identifiers that are used for wafer control inside the manufacturing equipment or used in the information the manufacturing equipment provides externally. Depending on the information source, the following generation methods are available:
(1) A method to use host-provided data:
The content map provided by the host is used as wafer-identifying data.
--[p27]-- (2) A method to use equipment-provided data:
If the host provides no wafer-identifying data, the production equipment generates identification data using carrier ID and slot number as content map information.

===== [p34-34] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3. Trigger for NPW use (2块) =====
--[p34]-- One representative example of the former is when there is an insufficient number of product wafers and dummy wafers are automatically inserted to make up for the deficit. ===== [p40-40] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 3.5. Carrier Loading/Unloading Detection Mechanism (1块) =====
--[p40]-- 3.5. Carrier Loading/Unloading Detection Mechanism
To enable the detection of the completion of loading/unloading a carrier, each loadport shall be provided with a carrier presence sensor and a carrier placement sensor. These sensors shall be independent each other logically as well as physically.
(a) Carrier presence sensor
Provides a mechanism to check whether a carrier is placed on the loadport.
(b) Carrier placement sensor
Provides a mechanism to check whether a carrier is placed on the loadport in the correct state.

===== [p24-24] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.4. Access Mode Management Units (2块) =====
--[p24]-- Loadports that are placed adjacently and share the same mode for carrier transportation constitute a loadport group. More than one loadport group is defined for each piece of equipment. Management is performed for each group.
--[p24]-- 2.1.1.4. Access Mode Management Units
Equipment, loadport or loadport group is used as a unit to manage access modes used for management of materials (carriers) -conveying means.
(1) With equipment for the unit:
A piece of equipment as a whole has an access mode. If the equipment has more than one loadports, the loadports have the same access mode
(2) With the loadport as the unit:
Each loadport has a specific access mode.
(3) With the loadport group as the unit:

===== [p10-10] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 1.2.2. Unit Cycle Size (2块) =====
--[p10]-- From the aspect of process throughput, however, it is interpreted that one or more wafers are held in each cycle or in the entire module.
The cluster tool and the coater/developer have the capacity to process multiple wafers at one time, but they process individual wafers separately.
--[p10]-- 1.2.2. Unit Cycle Size
The unit cycle size corresponds to execution of a single process for the entire set of equipment. The size is the unit number of materials to be processed in the execution of a single process. There are three basic types: single wafer, single-carrier multi-wafer batch, and multi-carrier batch.
· Single wafer:
Generally, the cluster tool and the coater/developer hold only a single wafer for each process module.

===== [p28-28] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > (1) Using the same carrier (1块) =====
--[p28]-- (1) Using the same carrier
The same carrier is used for both the preliminary and main processing.
The preliminary processing and the main processing are executed by different control jobs. After the preliminary processing is executed, the processed wafers return to the original carrier, which is then taken out of the equipment. Measurement and other jobs are executed at different points. After that, the equipment executes the main processing that is not executed in the preliminary processing.
The equipment is unconscious of the preliminary processing and the main processing.

===== [p170-171] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 5.1.5.4. Carrier ID Verification Failure (2块) =====
--[p171]-- Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor>
Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor>
--[p170]-- 5.1.5.4. Carrier ID Verification Failure
The following two carrier ID verification failure patterns are considered.
(1) Carrier ID reading failure
(2) Carrier ID inconsistency
In either case, continuing processing is possible even if an error is generated. However, basically the operator must intervene to check whether an abnormality is found in the carrier or the equipment.
Operation flows and scenarios to be used when an error is generated are described for the two patterns above.

===== [p34-34] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 6. Storage method (1块) =====
--[p34]-- 6. Storage method
The available NPW storage methods comprise the method in which the NPW wafers are loaded when the need arises and the method in which NPW wafers are stocked in the equipment in advance. It shall be indicated which of these two methods should be employed.

===== [p25-25] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > (1) Equipment verification: (1块) =====
--[p25]-- (1) Equipment verification:
The ID of a carrier to be loaded by the host is notified to the equipment in advance. If the arriving carrier is the one assumed to arrive, the equipment automatically identifies that the ID is correct, then accepting the carrier.
(2) Host verification:
In this method, the host does not inform the equipment of the carrier ID. A carrier ID read by the equipment is sent to the host, which decides whether the carrier should be accepted.

===== [p27-27] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.17. Process Starting Method (1块) =====
--[p27]-- 2.1.1.17. Process Starting Method
The process starting method is a way of specifying the timing to start a job (control job and process job). The timing can be specified when creating a job.
(1) Manual start : Service commands, executable at the host's discretion, are used to start processing.
(2) Automatic start : When process-executing resources become ready, processing is started automatically at equipment's discretion.

===== [p57-57] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.1.11. Collection of Variable Data (1块) =====
--[p57]-- 4.1.11. Collection of Variable Data
Equipment must meet requirements for the collection of variable data described in E30.

===== [p52-52] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4. Communcations Requirements (1块) =====
--[p52]-- 4. Communcations Requirements
Requirements for communications between the host and equipment are specified.

===== [p476-476] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > · Production Equipment (1块) =====
--[p476]-- · Production Equipment
Equipment used to manufacture products, including wafer sorters, process devices and measuring instruments, but not including material conveyors

===== [p478-478] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > · Resource (1块) =====
--[p478]-- · Resource
Resources to process materials (wafers), such as chambers and modules

===== [p25-25] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 2.1.1.10. FOUP Undocking Timings (1块) =====
--[p25]-- 2.1.1.10. FOUP Undocking Timings
After the processing of the wafers in a FOUP is completed, there are the following timings to undock the FOUP from the docked position.
(1) After processing has been completed:
Undocking is performed at the timing when the FOUP door is closed after processing has been completed.

===== [p478-478] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > ·Recipe (1块) =====
--[p478]-- ·Recipe
Prepared in advance and controlled by process execution resources using commands, set values and parameters that can be used repeatedly, in order to determine the process environment with regard to materials

===== [p58-58] Equipment Online Functionality Requirement Document <Volume for Fixed Buffer/Single Wafer Processor> > 4.1.14. Remote Control (1块) =====
--[p58]-- 4.1.14. Remote Control
Equipment must meet requirements for remote control described in E30.
